```
def _c5_fft(h, dx):
    n = len(h)
    L = 2*n - 1
    a = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    b = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - np.flip(h), L)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(a * b, L)
    # due to numerical, small where should zero
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)
```

```
def _corr_index_to_k(n):
    h = np.array([0.1, 0.7, 0.4, 0.9, 0.2][:n], dtype=float)
    vals = np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full')
    # compute pos and neg
    npos = np.empty(n)
    for k in range(n): npos[k] = np.dot(h[:n-k], 1-h[k:])
    nneg = np.empty(n-1)
    for r in range(1,n): nneg[r] = np.dot(h[r:], 1-h[:n-r])
    # try mapping k = n-1-p
    ok1 = True
    for p in range(2*n-1):
       k = n-1-p
       expected = npos[k] if k>=0 else nneg[-k]
       if abs(vals[p]-expected)>1e-9: ok1=False; break
    if ok1: return 1
    return -1
```

`_c5_fft`:
```
def _c5_fft(h, dx):
    n=len(h)
    M=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L < M: L <<= 1
    a = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    b = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(1.0 - h), L)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(a*b, L, norm=None)[:M]
    corr = np.real(corr)
    # numerical small negative; objective can't be negative
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)
```
Need `np.flip` each eval O(n), okay. Could precompute? no.

def _determine_corr_sign():
    n=8
    rng = np.random.RandomState(1); h = rng.rand(n); b=1-h
    vals = np.correlate(h,b,'full')
    pos = np.empty(n); neg=np.empty(n-1)
    for k in range(n): pos[k]=np.dot(h[:n-k], b[k:])
    for r in range(1,n): neg[r]=np.dot(h[r:], b[:n-r])
    # try k = n-1-p
    ok1=True
    for p in range(2*n-1):
        k=n-1-p
        exp=pos[k] if k>=0 else neg[-k]
        if abs(vals[p]-exp)>1e-10: ok1=False; break
    if ok1: return 1
    # try k = p-(n-1)
    return -1
```

Projection exact function:
```
def project_exact(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h,float)
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # If sum already close, fine; else distribute.
    for _ in range(20):
       s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff > 0:
          mask = h < 1-1e-14
          if not mask.any():
             # increase all by diff/n if small? might exceed 1; but target<=n; should not if h<=1 and sum<target
          delta = diff / mask.sum()
          h[mask] = np.minimum(1.0, h[mask]+delta)
       else:
          mask = h > 1e-14
          if not mask.any(): break
          delta = diff / mask.sum()
          h[mask] = np.maximum(0.0, h[mask]+delta)
    # final residue adjust one index with slack
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       idxs = np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
       if idxs.size==0:
          idxs = np.where(h<1)[0] if diff>0 else np.where(h>0)[0]
       if idxs.size:
          j=idxs[np.argmin(np.abs(h[idxs]-0.5))?]
          h[j] = np.clip(h[j]+diff,0,1)
    return h
```
But due to float sum still not exact. We can do more robust exact using sorted slack one time:

Define:
```
def enforce_sum(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h,float)
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(10):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-14
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-14
        if not np.any(mask): break
        h[mask] += diff / mask.sum()
        h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        # find index with room
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.argmax(1.0 - h)
            h[idx] = np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1)
        else:
            idx = np.argmin(h)
            h[idx] = np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1)
    # correct residual by adjusting adjacent maybe
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       # use integer-ish set? ignore
    return h
```

Quantize function:
```
def quantize_exact(h, target, q=16384):
    hi = np.rint(h * q).astype(np.int64)
    hi = np.clip(hi,0,q)
    rem = int(round(target*q)) - int(hi.sum())
    # Need adjust by rem, can add/remove in chunks of 1 to random indices with room
    if rem >0:
        order = np.argsort(q - hi)  # most slack first
        for idx in order:
            add = min(int(q - hi[idx]), rem)
            hi[idx] += add; rem -= add
            if rem==0: break
    elif rem<0:
        order = np.argsort(hi) # least first? subtract from small to avoid negative? Actually need subtract total -rem, choose high first?
        rem = -rem
        for idx in np.argsort(-hi):
            sub = min(int(hi[idx]), rem)
            hi[idx] -= sub; rem -= sub
            if rem==0: break
    # If rem remains (shouldn't if target within [0,n]), adjust maybe by changing one large
    return hi.astype(float)/q
```

def get_top_shifts(h, k_count, tol=0.0):
    n=len(h)
    vals = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') if n<=600 else _c5_values_fft(h)
    idx = np.argpartition(vals, -k_count)[-k_count:]
    shifts=[]
    for p in idx:
       k = CORR_K_SIGN * (n-1-p)
       shifts.append(int(k))
    return shifts, vals[idx]
```

LP function:
```
def one_lp_step(h, active_k, active_vals, max_active=60):
    n=len(h)
    # sort active by descending val maybe
    # Build A rows
    A=[]; b=[]
    for k in active_k:
       g=grad_for_shift(h,k)
       row = np.empty(n+1); row[:n]=g; row[-1]=-1.0
       A.append(row)
    A=np.array(A)
    b=np.zeros(len(A))
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1; b_eq=np.zeros(1)
    c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1
    bounds=[( -h[i], 1-h[i]) for i in range(n)] + [( -10, 1)]
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, Aeq=Aeq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8})
```

def try_delta(h, delta, cur_val, target):
   # ensure delta sum zero? maybe delta -= mean(delta)
   dsum = delta.sum()
   if abs(dsum)>1e-10: delta = delta - dsum/len(delta)
   # clip based on h bounds: actual max lambda
   lam_candidates = [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
   # Compute max feasible lam = 1? because delta bounds from LP, but after mean correction may violate; compute lam_max = min over i with delta>0 (1-h)/delta, delta<0 (h)/(-delta), plus 1 maybe
   best_val=cur_val; best_h=h
   for lam in lam_candidates:
       if lam > lam_max: continue
       cand = h + lam*delta
       cand = enforce_sum(cand,target)
       # maybe quantize? no
       val = evaluate(cand)
       if val < best_val - 1e-12:
           best_val=val; best_h=cand
   return best_h,best_val
```

      vb=eval

      # if no_progress > threshold: restart from h with noise:

Need get sorted shifts. Implement:
```
def top_shifts_sorted(h, max_count=200):
    n=len(h)
    if n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
       vals = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    else:
       vals = _c5_values_fft(h)
    count = min(max_count, len(vals))
    part = np.argpartition(vals, -count)[-count:]
    part = part[np.argsort(vals[part])[::-1]]
    shifts = [CORR_K_SIGN * (n-1-p) for p in part]
    return shifts, vals
```

Function `smoothed_gradient(h, tau)`:
- vals = correlate values (correct orientation)
- maxv = vals.max()
- z = (vals - maxv)/tau
- w = exp(z - max(z)) / sum
- grad = zeros(n)
- For each p, if w[p]>1e-10: k=sign*(n-1-p); grad += w[p]*grad_for_shift(h,k)
- grad -= mean(grad) for sum constraint.
- Optionally enforce bounds via projected gradient: `h_new = h - step*grad`; `enforce_sum`; but box projection after step can use `np.clip` then enforce. However if h near bounds, gradient descent should respect. Could use adaptive step.

def _c5_values_fft(h):
    n=len(h); M=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L < M: L <<= 1
    a = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    b = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(1.0-h), L)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(a*b, L, norm=None)[:M]
    return corr

Line search for gradient:
```
def try_direction(h, direction, cur_val, target, scales=(...)):
   n=len(h)
   dsum = direction.sum()
   if abs(dsum)>1e-12: direction = direction - dsum/n
   # feasible max lam
   lam_max=1.0
   pos = direction > 1e-14
   if pos.any(): lam_max = min(lam_max, np.min((1-h[pos])/direction[pos]))
   neg = direction < -1e-14
   if neg.any(): lam_max = min(lam_max, np.min((-h[neg])/(-direction[neg]))) # since direction negative, h / -direction
   # candidate lambdas descending up to lam_max
   best_h=h; best_val=cur_val
   for base in [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002]:
       lam = base * lam_max if lam_max <1 else base
       if lam <=0: continue
       cand = h + lam*direction
       cand = project? enforce_sum
       val = evaluate_raw(cand)
       if val < best_val - 1e-12:
           best_val=val; best_h=cand
           # can break? We want best among tried; but if decreasing, likely largest improvement at largest? not necessarily. Keep search.
   return best_h,best_val
```

```
def lp_line_search(h, active_shifts, cur_val, target):
    n=len(h)
    if not active_shifts: return h,cur_val
    A_rows=[]
    for k in active_shifts:
       g = grad_for_shift(h,k)
       row = np.empty(n+1); row[:n]=g; row[-1]=-1.0
       A_rows.append(row)
    A=np.vstack(A_rows)
    b=np.zeros(A.shape[0])
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
    c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
    lb=np.empty(n); ub=np.empty(n)
    for i in range(n): lb[i]=-h[i]; ub[i]=1-h[i]
    bounds=list(zip(lb,ub)) + [(-1.0, 1.0)] # t can be maybe -1? Actually if constraints allow t < -1, objective unbounded? finite but maybe t<-1? f values ~1; linearized decrease within δ box max change maybe n, but t lower bounded by min possible linear values maybe -n. Set bound (-10,1). To avoid restricting, use (-100,100) or (-inf,inf). Bounds lower can be -100. Use (-100,100).
    try:
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, Aeq=Aeq, b_eq=np.zeros(1), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'disp':False})
       if not res.success: return h,cur_val
       delta=res.x[:n]
    except Exception:
       return h,cur_val
    # remove numerical t effect? delta sum may not zero. Correct.
    s=delta.sum(); delta -= s/n
    return try_line_search(h, delta, cur_val, target, [1.0,...])
```
Need note LP objective may return t not 0; δ feasible? since original b=0 corresponds δ=0,t=0; LP feasible. But after line search, δ may not sum 0 exactly. We correct, may violate constraints; line search project.
Bounds: For delta, lower = -h -1e-9? Because if h zero, LB -0; line search can't go below. Use `-h[i] - 1e-9`, `1-h[i] + 1e-9` allow slight, then project. But if allow, λ=1 may exceed; line search compute lam_max based on h and delta after correction. Better set exact and lam_max=1; after mean correction may violate. Use lam_max as above. Good.
But correcting sum may change direction; use direction = delta - mean. Then lam_max finite. Good.

def polish(h, t_start, t_end, seed):
   n=len(h); target=n/2.0
   h=np.project...
   cur=evaluate_raw(h)
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   tau=0.08
   no_smooth=0; no_lp=0
   # cache shift_list
   while time.time() < t_end:
      # 1 smooth gradient
      try: g, maxv = smooth_gradient(h, tau)
      except: g=None
      if g is not None:
         g_scaled = g / (np.max(np.abs(g))+1e-12)
         step = -g_scaled
         h2,val2 = try_line_search(h, step, cur, target, scales=[0.1,0.2,0.05,...])
         if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
             h,val2= h2; cur=val2; no_smooth=0; tau=max(0.005, tau*0.95) if cur < record? maybe
             continue
      no_smooth +=1
      if no_smooth % 3 ==0:
          # add small random perturbation? maybe after 5 no smooth
          if no_smooth > 5:
             h2 = project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0, 0.05, n), target)
             val2=eval
             if <cur: accept
             tau *=1.2? or reduce
      # LP with top active shifts
      shifts, vals = top_shifts_sorted(h, 100)
      active_choices = []
      for c in [1,3,5,10,20,50]: active_choices.append(shifts[:c])
      # if n small, all
      if n <= 120: active_choices.append(shifts) # all? top 100 covers maybe all if M<=100; if n=70 M=139, need shifts all: top_count=M. changes.
      # We'll use top_shifts_sorted(h, M) for all for n<=100

         if time.time() > t_end: break
         h2,val2 = lp_line_search(h, active, cur, target)
         if val2 < cur -1e-12:
            h,val = h2,val2; cur=val; no_lp=0; no_smooth=0; continue
      no_lp +=1
      if no_lp > 8:
         # random pair move
         # choose 2 indices, random d
         for _ in range(20):
            i,j = rng.choice(n, size=2, replace=False)
            maxd = min(1-h[i], h[j])? For moving mass? Let's do independent perturb preserving sum:
            d = rng.uniform(-0.2,0.2)
            cand=h.copy(); cand[i]+=d; cand[j]-=d
            cand=project_box_sum(cand,target); val=eval
            if val < cur: accept
         if no improve:
            # try binary threshold
            hb = threshold_impulse(h,target)
            vb=eval
            if vb<cur: accept
            # try random continuous restart from current with noise or from best of pool
            # If time remains, break to outer candidate loop
         no_lp=0

Detailed function implementations:

`enforce_sum`:
```
def enforce_sum(h, target, tol=1e-12):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h[:] = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # distribute residual evenly among non-bound entries up to 30 iterations
    for _ in range(30):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= tol:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-13
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-13
        if not np.any(mask):
            break
        delta = diff / float(np.sum(mask))
        h[mask] += delta
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    # final small adjustment
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > tol:
        if diff > 0:
            # choose index with largest headroom
            room = 1.0 - h
            idx = int(np.argmax(room))
            add = min(diff, room[idx])
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
            h[idx] -= sub
    # If still off, distribute over indices not at boundary maybe
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        # as last resort adjust all by diff/n, clip
        h = h + diff/len(h)
        np.clip(h,0.1? no, out)
    return h
```
This may leave sum off tiny. Fine.
For final quantize exact.

But in line search, we call enforce_sum frequently; 30 loops updating sum via np.sum O(n) maybe okay. We can optimize: after adding small δ sum known? Not needed for n=70 and thousands maybe okay. But `np.sum` O(n) plus mask. We can improve:
```
def enforce_sum_fast(h, target):
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    # if all not at bounds, one-step: h += diff/n; if no clip, good.
    if abs(diff) < 1e-13: return h
    h2 = h + diff/n
    if np.all((h2>=0)&(h2<=1)): return h2
    # else loop
```
Most line steps small, one-step works. Use:
```
def enforce_sum(h,target,tol=1e-12):
    h = np.array(h,float,copy=True)
    np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    n=h.size
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) <= tol: return h
    h2 = h + diff/n
    if h2.min() >=0 and h2.max() <=1:
        return h2
    # loop
```
This is faster. For final maybe use robust loop. We can combine.

`threshold_impulse`:
```
def threshold_impulse(h, target):
    n=len(h)
    m=int(round(target))
    if m <=0: hcb=np.zeros(n)
    elif m>=n: hcb=np.ones(n)
    else:
       idx = np.argsort(-h)  # descending
       hcb=np.zeros(n)
       hcb[idx[:m]]=1.0
    return hcb
```

`quantize_exact`:
```
def quantize_exact(h, target, q=1<<20):
    # choose q based on n
    n=len(h)
    if n > 4096: q=1<<14
    hi = np.rint(np.clip(h,0,1) * q).astype(np.int64)
    target_q = int(round(target * q))
    rem = target_q - int(np.sum(hi))
    if rem > 0:
       # sort indices by descending room
       if rem: 
          order = np.argsort(-(q - hi))  # can't use unless? okay
          for idx in order:
             if rem <=0: break
             add = min(int(q - hi[idx]), rem)
             if add: hi[idx]+=add; rem-=add
    elif rem <0:
       rem = -rem
       order = np.argsort(-hi) # largest first to subtract
       for idx in order:
          if rem<=0: break
          sub = min(int(hi[idx]), rem)
          if sub: hi[idx]-=sub; rem-=sub
    # if rem not zero (shouldn't), adjust first with room
    if rem >0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(q-hi)); hi[idx]=q
    elif rem<0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(hi)); hi[idx]=0
    out = hi.astype(float) / float(q)
    # ensure sum target exact? floating should.
    return out
```
If target not integer? We choose n even, target integer. Initial parent n=70 target 35. If some n odd, even? We will only process even. But quantize with float target maybe non-integer; target_q int round. Good.
But sorting every candidate final okay. For continuous, if hi sum target_q exactly after adjustments. Since q power of two, out sum exact (if no overflow). Need `np.sum(h)` for eval may be exact? It sums dyadic. Good.

Potential problem: `np.rint(h*q)` can produce hi sum far from target; if rem large (e.g., 5000) loop over many indices. n small. Fine.

def make_lowpass(n, rng, bandwidth=6, alpha=0.9, amp=0.48):
   u=np.zeros(n)
   for f in range(1, bandwidth+1):
      c = rng.uniform(-1,1); s=rng.uniform(-1,1)
      scale = amp / (f ** alpha)
      k = (2*np.pi*f*np.arange(n))/n
      u += c*scale*np.cos(k) + s*(scale*0.5)*np.sin(k)

`try_line_search` implementation:

def try_line_search(h, direction, cur_val, target, n_scales=6):
    n=h.size
    direction = direction - direction.mean()
    # if direction norm zero return
    scale = np.max(np.abs(direction))
    if scale < 1e-14: return h,cur_val
    direction = direction / scale  # unit max coordinate
    # feasible lam for unit direction
    lam_max=1.0
    pos = direction > 0
    if np.any(pos):
        room = 1.0 - h[pos]
        maxroom = np.min(room/direction[pos]) # direction positive
        if maxroom < lam_max: lam_max=maxroom
    neg = direction < 0
    if np.any(neg):
        room = h[pos? negative]
        # direction negative, maximum decrease = h / (-direction)
        val = np.min(h[neg] / (-direction[neg]))
        if val < lam_max: lam_max=val
    if lam_max <=0: return...
    # Candidate lambdas: fractions of lam_max
    fracs = [1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125]
    best... 
    for f in fracs:
       lam = f*lam_max
       if lam <=0: continue
       cand=h + lam*direction
       # Because direction normalized, lam is coordinate max change.
       cand = enforce_sum(cand,target) # after sum correction sum should be target but enforce tiny
       val=evaluate_raw(cand)
       if val < best_val - 1e-12:
          best_val=val; best_h=cand
          # If f==1 and improved? Could not break; maybe check smaller if larger not best. Continue.
    return best_h,best_val
```

def smooth_state(h, tau):
   vals = _c5_values(h)
   maxv = float(np.max(vals))
   z = (vals - maxv)/tau
   z -= np.max(z)
   w = np.exp(z)
   sw = w.sum()

   skips = w < 1e-8

def polish(h0, t_end, seed, verbose=False):
   h = project_box_sum(h0, len(h0)/2.0)
   cur = evaluate_raw(h)
   rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
   tau = 0.08
   no_improve_smooth = 0
   no_improve_lp = 0
   n = len(h); target=n/2.0
   last_random = time.time()
   while time.time() < t_end:
      # if cur below lower bound maybe break
      if cur < 0.3745:
         break
      improved = False
      # smooth gradient
      if time.time() < t_end - 1:
         try:
            g, maxv = smooth_gradient(h, tau)
            h2, val2 = try_step_projected(h, g, cur, target)
            if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2; cur = val2; improved=True; no_improve_smooth=0
                # maybe tau *=0.97
                tau = max(0.004, tau*0.97 if val2 < maxv else tau)
                continue
         except Exception:
            pass
      no_improve_smooth += 1
      if no_improve_smooth > 8:
         # random perturbation smooth scale
         if time.time() - last_random > 5: # not top-level? time okay
             noise = 0.03 + 0.05*min(1.0, no_improve_smooth/10)
             h2 = project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0, noise, n), target)
             val2 = evaluate_raw(h2)
             if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                 h=h2; cur=val2; improved=True; no_improve_smooth=0
                 last_random=time.time()
              else:
                 # reduce tau? Actually reduce tau to focus, or increase? If stuck, try larger tau to escape.
                 tau = min(0.3, tau*1.5)
         no_improve_smooth = 0? maybe
      # LP attempts
      M=2*n-1
      if n <= 120:
         counts = [1,3,5,10,20,50,M]
      elif n <= 260:
         counts = [1,3,5,10,20,50]
      else:
         counts = [1,3,5,10]
      shifts, vals = top_shifts_sorted(h, min(counts[-1], M))
      # Need for each count, if count > len shifts get all
      for c in counts:
         if time.time() > t_end: break
         if c > len(shifts): c=len(shifts)
         active = shifts[:c]
         h2, val2 = lp_line_search(h, active, cur, target)
         if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
            h=h2; cur=val2; improved=True; no_improve_lp=0; no_improve_smooth=0
            # maybe anneal tau smaller
            tau = max(0.003, tau*0.9)
            break
      if improved: continue
      no_improve_lp += 1
      # if few LP improvements, binary threshold
      if no_improve_lp % 5 == 0:
         hb = threshold_impulse(h, target)
         vb = evaluate_raw(hb)
         if vb < cur - 1e-12:
             h=hb; cur=vb; improved=False? continue
      if no_improve_lp > 20:
         # random restart from h with larger noise
         for noise in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35]:
             h2 = project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0, noise, n), target)
             val2=evaluate_raw(h2)
             if val2 < cur -1e-12:
                 h=h2; cur=val2; break
         # reset counters; if still stuck, reduce LP active all? maybe return
         no_improve_lp=0
         no_improve_smooth=0
         tau=0.15
         # To avoid infinite same, random pair? If no improvement after multiple restarts maybe break
         if val2 >= cur: # not improved
             # try pool? Here can't access. Just break after count?
             # We'll break if a global `streak` >? But function doesn't know.
             pass
      if time.time() > t_end: break
   return h, cur
```

`make_lowpass_candidate(n, rng, bandwidth, alpha, amp)`:
```
x=np.arange(n)
u=np.zeros(n)
for f in range(1, int(bandwidth)+1):
    ang = 2*np.pi*f*x/n
    c = rng.normal(0,1)
    s = rng.normal(0,1)
    sc = 1.0/(f**alpha)
    u += c*sc*np.cos(ang) + s*sc*0.7*np.sin(ang)
mx = np.max(np.abs(u))
if mx < 1e-12: u = rng.random_sample(n) - 0.5
u = 0.5 * amp * u / mx  # max amp (<=0.5)
h = 0.5 + u
return project_box_sum(h, n/2.0)
```

def add_candidate(pool, h, current_best):
   h = np.asarray(h,float)
   if h.ndim !=1 or h.size==0 or h.size %2 !=0: return current_best
   n=h.size
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return
   h = np.clip(h,0,1)
   h = enforce_sum(h, n/2.0)
   val = evaluate_raw(h)
   if np.isfinite(val) and val < current_best + 0.1: # keep near-best
       pool.append((val,h))
   # also if current_best inf
   return val if val < current_best else current_best
```

`_determine_corr_sign` at import:
```
def _determine_corr_sign():
    n=6
    h=np.array([0.3,0.9,0.1,0.6,0.4,0.2], dtype=float)
    b=1.0-h
    vals=np.correlate(h,b,mode='full')
    # compute expected mapping k = n-1-p
    ok=True
    for p in range(2*n-1):
       k=n-1-p
       if k>=0:
          exp=np.dot(h[:n-k], b[k:])
       else:
          r=-k
          exp=np.dot(h[r:], b[:n-r])
       if abs(vals[p]-exp)>1e-8:
          ok=False; break
    if ok: return 1
    return -1
CORR_K_SIGN = _determine_corr_sign()
```
If it returns -1, formula should be k = -(n-1-p). But if unknown, maybe try both. Could implement `get_shift_list` testing both? Simpler: after computing sign, if sign -1, shift list accordingly. We trust.

`get_shift_list(n)`:
```
def get_shift_list(n):
    if n not in SHIFT_CACHE:
       shifts = [0]*(2*n-1)
       for p in range(2*n-1):
           shifts[p] = int(CORR_K_SIGN * (n - 1 - p))
       SHIFT_CACHE[n] = shifts
    return SHIFT_CACHE[n]
```
If CORR_K_SIGN not set? top-level set.

`enforce_sum` implementation robust:
```
def enforce_sum(h, target, tol=1e-12):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if h.size == 0: return h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    n = h.size
    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= tol:
            break
        # If adding diff/n keeps in bounds, do it
        # But if some at bounds, need redistribution.
        step = diff / n
        candidates = h + step
        if candidates.min() >= 0.0 and candidates.max() <= 1.0:
            h = candidates
            continue
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-14
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-14
        if not np.any(mask):
            break
        count = int(np.sum(mask))
        delta = diff / count
        h[mask] = h[mask] + delta
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            idx = int(np.argmax(room))
            if room[idx] > 0:
                h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            if h[idx] > 0:
                h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h
```
If h+step overflows? okay.
Could loop infinite if mask full and diff/n invalid due to one bound? If mask full but candidate invalid means some negative/above; but if all h<1 for diff>0, candidate max might exceed if step big? But diff/n valid because total capacity enough? If all <1, h+diff/n could still exceed 1 for those near 1 if diff/n large? Example h=[0.99,0], target=1, diff=0.01, step=0.005, candidate [0.995,0.005] valid. In general if all <1, average step to raise sum; some may exceed if one already high, but if all <1, step <= (1 - max)? Not necessarily: h=[0.99,0,0], n=3, target=1.5? diff=0? Example target sum larger, step maybe > capacity of high? Suppose h=[0.999,0,0], target=1.2, diff=0.201, step=0.067, candidate max 1.066 invalid. Mask all <1, delta=0.201, but first capacity 0.001, others 1. Adding 0.201 to all invalid. Clip reduces, loops. Eventually distribute to non-bound. The loop may converge slowly if many? It clips, then mask excludes saturated, delta bigger. 30 iterations enough.
Could improve by water-filling, but okay.

`evaluate_raw`:
```
def evaluate_raw(h):
    n = h.size
    if n == 0: return float('inf')
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    if n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        return float(np.max(corr) * dx)
    return _c5_fft(h, dx)
```
Could h contain NaN? ensure finite. If not, return inf.
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return inf
```
But `np.all` many. Evaluate called often. Maybe assume finite after project. But for safety in generation only. We can skip.

`_c5_fft`:
```
def _c5_fft(h, dx):
    n=h.size
    M=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L < M:
        L <<= 1
    a = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    b = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(1.0 - h), L)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(a * b, L)
    corr = corr[:M]
    # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
    mx = float(np.max(corr))
    if mx < 0.0: mx = 0.0
    return mx * dx
```
`np.flip(1.0-h)` creates reversed complement. Good.
Could `a*b` complex; irfft uses only first L//2+1; okay.

`_c5_values`:
```
def _c5_values(h):
    n=h.size
    if n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    M=2*n-1; L=1; while L<M: L<<=1
    a=np.fft.rfft(h,L); b=np.fft.rfft(np.flip(1.0-h),L)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(a*b,L)[:M]
    # clip small negative?
    corr[corr < 0] = 0? But small negative might affect sorting? Values nonnegative. Do `np.maximum(corr,0,out=corr)`.
    return corr
```
For FFT values, small negative; set to 0 for top. But if all values true small? no.

`top_shifts_from_vals(vals, n, count)`:
```
def top_shifts_from_vals(vals, n, count):
    M=len(vals)
    if count <=0: return []
    if count >= M:
       idx=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(vals, -count)[-count:]
       idx=idx[np.argsort(vals[idx])[::-1]]
    shifts=get_shift_list(n)
    out=[]
    for p in idx:
       out.append(shifts[int(p)])
    return out
```
If count > M, argsort full. Good.

`weighted_gradient_fvals(h, fvals, tau)`:
```
def weighted_gradient_fvals(h, fvals, tau):
    n=h.size
    if tau <= 0: tau=1e-6
    maxv = float(np.max(fvals))
    z = (fvals - maxv) / tau
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(z - zmax)
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if sw <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
       # uniform over max
       w = np.zeros(len(fvals)); w[np.argmax(fvals)] = 1.0
    else:
       w /= sw
    shifts = get_shift_list(n)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    # iterate
    for p in range(len(w)):
       wp = float(w[p])
       if wp > 1e-10:
          g += wp * grad_for_shift(h, shifts[p])
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    return g
```
`fvals` length M. If tau small, w sparse. Good.

`try_step_projected`:
```
def try_step_projected(h, direction, cur_val, target):
    n=h.size
    if direction.size != n: return h, cur_val
    direction = np.array(direction, dtype=float, copy=True)
    # remove mean to preserve sum
    direction -= float(np.mean(direction))
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(direction))) if n else 0.0
    if maxd < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(maxd):
        return h, cur_val
    direction /= maxd
    # re-center after normalization? mean may be small; subtract mean and renormalize
    direction -= float(np.mean(direction))
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(direction)))
    if maxd < 1e-14: return h,cur_val
    direction /= maxd
    # compute feasible lam
    lam_max = 1.0
    pos = direction > 0.0
    if np.any(pos):
        # need finite
        frac = (1.0 - h[pos]) / direction[pos]
        m = float(np.min(frac[np.isfinite(frac)])) if np.any(np.isfinite(frac)) else 0.0
        if m < lam_max: lam_max = m
    neg = direction < 0.0
    if np.any(neg):
        frac = h[neg] / (-direction[neg])
        m = float(np.min(frac[np.isfinite(frac)])) if np.any(np.isfinite(frac)) else 0.0
        if m < lam_max: lam_max = m
    if lam_max <= 1e-12:
        return h, cur_val
    best_val = cur_val
    best_h = h
    fracs = [1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.15, 0.1, 0.05, 0.025, 0.01]
    # include lam_max? fracs relative; if lam_max < 1, steps = frac*lam_max.
    # if lam_max not in fracs maybe add sorted unique [lam_max,0.5, ...] but 1 step already full feasible. Good.
    for f in fracs:
        lam = f * lam_max
        if lam <= 0: continue
        cand = h + lam * direction
        # ensure exact sum and bounds maybe
        cand = enforce_sum(cand, target)
        # due to enforce, may not be feasible? clip enforced.
        val = evaluate_raw(cand)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val - 1e-12:
            best_val = val
            best_h = cand
    return best_h, best_val
```
If direction normalized and lam=1 moves possible to boundary; if original direction was LP δ with max 0.3, this tries max change up to 1, larger than original; maybe good. Could find new. But might overshoot. Smaller included. Fine.
But if `enforce_sum` clips after `h+lam*direction`, if lam was feasible before, no clip; after mean correction? direction sum zero; enforce no change. Good.
If evaluate val is NaN due to ??? skip.

`lp_line_search`:
```
def lp_line_search(h, active_shifts, active_vals, cur_val, target):
    # active_vals are counts or objective? Use counts. If pass fvals maybe use fvals? The direction independent but t bound. Let's pass count vals to align gradient.
    n=h.size
    if len(active_shifts)==0: return h,cur_val
    M=len(active_shifts)
    A = np.zeros((M, n+1))
    b = np.zeros(M)
    for i in range(M):
        g = grad_for_shift(h, active_shifts[i])
        A[i,:n] = g
        A[i,n] = -1.0
        b[i] = -float(active_vals[i])
    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n+1)); Aeq[0,:n] = 1.0
    c = np.zeros(n+1); c[n] = 1.0
    bounds = []
    for j in range(n):
        bounds.append(( -float(h[j]), 1.0 - float(h[j]) ))
    bounds.append((0.0, 2.0 * float(n) + 1.0)) # t count bounds
    try:
        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, Aeq=Aeq, b_eq=np.zeros(1), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'disp': False})
        if res.success is False:
            return h, cur_val
        delta = np.array(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return h, cur_val
    # If delta all near zero, try negative? Actually LP minimizes max count; maybe delta could be zero if no descent? return.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)) or np.max(np.abs(delta)) < 1e-13:
        return h, cur_val
    # The LP t optimum maybe not lower? line search
    h2, val2 = try_step_projected(h, delta, cur_val, target)
    # Also try -delta? LP direction could be zero-sum? Try if not improved maybe -delta? But -delta likely increases. optional.
    if val2 >= cur_val - 1e-12:
        h2b, val2b = try_step_projected(h, -delta, cur_val, target)
        if val2b < val2:
            h2, val2 = h2b, val2b
    return h2, val2
```
Note active_vals are counts? If using counts, at δ=0 constraints t ≥ count max. t bound upper 2n+1. If active_vals are objective (divided by dx), constraint f_obj + (dx*grad)δ? Not match. So pass counts `vals[p]`. In polish, top shifts sorted by fvals, but retrieve count vals = `vals_count[p]`. We can have top function return positions. `polish` detailed with positions:
```
def polish(h0, t_end, seed):
    n=len(h0)
    target = float(n)//2? For even n/2. Use target = float(n)/2.0.
    h = enforce_sum(h0, target)
    cur = evaluate_raw(h)
    if not finite: return h0, cur? maybe.
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    tau = 0.08
    no_smooth = 0
    no_lp = 0
    rounds_with_no_improve = 0
    # maybe store best local? return best within polish? If random change worsens then later? We always only accept improvements, so h is monotonic. Good.
    while time.time() < t_end:
        if time.time() > t_end: break
        improved = False
        # compute vals
        vals_count = _c5_values(h)
        dx = 2.0 / n
        vals_obj = vals_count * dx
        cur = float(np.max(vals_obj))  # should equal evaluate
        # Smooth
        if time.time() < t_end - 0.1:
            g = weighted_gradient_fvals(h, vals_obj, tau)
            h2, val2 = try_step_projected(h, -g, cur, target)
            if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2; cur = val2; improved = True; no_smooth=0; no_lp=0
                # reduce tau a bit
                if tau > 0.004:
                    tau = max(0.004, tau * 0.95)
                continue
        no_smooth += 1
        if no_smooth >= 6:
            # random small perturbation
            noise = 0.02 + 0.03 * min(1.0, no_smooth / 10.0)
            h2 = project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), target)
            val2 = evaluate_raw(h2)
            if np.isfinite(val2) and val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2; cur = val2; improved = True; no_smooth=0; no_lp=0
                continue
            # occasionally threshold
            hb = threshold_impulse(h, target)
            vb = evaluate_raw(hb)
            if vb < cur - 1e-12:
                h = hb; cur = vb; improved = True; no_smooth=0; no_lp=0
                continue
            if no_smooth % 6 == 0:
                tau = min(0.25, tau * 1.3)
            no_smooth = 0
        # LP
        M = 2*n - 1
        if n <= 120:
            max_count = M
            counts = [1, 3, 5, 10, 20, 40, M]
        elif n <= 260:
            max_count = 80
            counts = [1, 3, 5, 10, 20, 40, 60]
        else:
            max_count = 40
            counts = [1, 3, 5, 10, 25]
        # remove duplicates sorted
        pos_all = top_positions_from_vals(vals_obj, max_count)
        # We need positions for each count
        seen_counts=[]
        for c in counts:
            cc = min(c, len(pos_all))
            if cc not in seen_counts: seen_counts.append(cc)
        for cc in seen_counts:
            if time.time() > t_end: break
            positions = pos_all[:cc]
            shifts = get_shift_list(n)
            active_shifts = [int(shifts[p]) for p in positions]
            active_counts = [float(vals_count[p]) for p in positions]
            h2, val2 = lp_line_search(h, active_shifts, active_counts, cur, target)
            if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2; cur = val2; improved = True; no_smooth=0; no_lp=0
                tau = max(0.003, tau * 0.9)
                break
        if not improved:
            no_lp += 1
            rounds_with_no_improve += 1
            if no_lp % 10 == 0:
                # binary threshold
                hb = threshold_impulse(h, target)
                vb = evaluate_raw(hb)
                if vb < cur - 1e-12:
                    h=hb; cur=vb; improved=True; no_lp=0; rounds_with_no_improve=0
            if rounds_with_no_improve > 150:
                # try larger random restart, if no success break
                improved2=False
                for noise in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]:
                    h2 = project_box_sum(h + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), target)
                    val2 = evaluate_raw(h2)
                    if np.isfinite(val2) and val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                        h=h2; cur=val2; improved=True; improved2=True
                        break
                if not improved2:
                    # after multiple failed, maybe lower tau and try; if too many, break
                    tau = 0.15
                    rounds_with_no_improve = 0
                    no_lp = 0
                    no_smooth = 0
                    # as a safety, break after a few full failed cycles
                    if rng.rand() < 0.3: # deterministic? no but okay
                        # do another cycle? We can break to free time for other candidates
                        break
        # if time low break
    return h, evaluate_raw(h)
```

`pool_add` function:
```
def pool_add(pool, h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.ndim != 1 or h.size == 0 or (h.size % 2 != 0): return
    n = h.size
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return
    if h.min() < -1e-6 or h.max() > 1.0 + 1e-6:
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = enforce_sum(h, float(n)/2.0)
    val = evaluate_raw(h)
    if np.isfinite(val):
        pool.append((val, h, n))
        if len(pool) > 300:
            prune_pool(pool, 200)
```
But if candidate n large, evaluating in pool_add many times. OK.

Need avoid using `h` from parent, we project and store. Good.

`interpolate_parent` function:
```
def interpolate_parent(parent, n):
    n0 = parent.size
    if n0 == n: return np.array(parent, dtype=float, copy=True)
    x0 = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n0)
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, parent)
```
If x1 outside? no.

`make_lowpass_candidate` uses `np.pi`.

`make_random_uniform` uses `rng.random_sample(n)`.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    # use budget_s float
    try:
       b = float(budget_s)
    except:
       b=1300.0
    if b <= 10: b = 10.0
    deadline = t0 + b - 20.0
    if deadline < t0 + 5.0: deadline = t0 + max(1.0, b*0.9)
    seed_int = int(seed) if seed is not None else 42
    pool = []
    parent = None
    try:
       initial_h_values_local = initial_h_values
    except Exception:
       initial_h_values_local = None
    if initial_h_values_local is not None:
       par = np.asarray(initial_h_values_local, dtype=float)
       if par.ndim == 1 and par.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(par)):
           if par.size % 2 != 0:
               # interpolate to even
               par = interpolate_parent(par, par.size + 1)
           parent = par
    best_val_parent = float('inf')
    if parent is not None:
       n0 = parent.size
       hpar = enforce_sum(parent, float(n0)/2.0)
       vpar = evaluate_raw(hpar)
       if np.isfinite(vpar): pool_add(pool,hpar)
       hth = threshold_impulse(parent, float(n0)/2.0)
       pool_add(pool,hth)
       hq = quantize_exact(hpar, float(n0)/2.0)
       pool_add(pool,hq)
```

Now, `polish` stall logic exact:
```
def polish(h0, t_end, seed):
    n = h0.size
    target = float(n)/2.0
    h = enforce_sum(h0, target)
    cur = evaluate_raw(h)
    if not np.isfinite(cur): return h, cur
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed % (2**31-1))
    tau = 0.08
    stall = 0
    while time.time() < t_end:
        if time.time() >= t_end: break
        vals_count = _c5_values(h)
        if vals_count.size != 2*n-1: break
        dx = 2.0 / float(n)
        vals_obj = vals_count * dx
        vals_obj = np.where(np.isfinite(vals_obj), vals_obj, -1.0)
        cur = float(np.max(vals_obj))
        if not np.isfinite(cur): break
        improved = False
        # Smooth
        if time.time() < t_end - 0.5:
            g = weighted_gradient_fvals(h, vals_obj, tau)
            h2, val2 = try_step_projected(h, -g, cur, target, steps=SMOOTH_STEPS)
            if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2; cur = val2; stall = 0; tau = max(0.004, tau * 0.96)
                continue
        # random small if stall > 2
        if stall > 2 and (stall % 5 == 0 or stall > 20):
            noise = 0.02 + 0.04 * min(1.0, stall / 20.0)
            h2 = enforce_sum(h + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), target)
            val2 = evaluate_raw(h2)
            if np.isfinite(val2) and val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2; cur = val2; stall = 0; continue
            # threshold
            ht = threshold_impulse(h, target)
            vt = evaluate_raw(ht)
            if vt < cur - 1e-12:
                h = ht; cur = vt; stall = 0; continue
            if stall % 10 == 0:
                tau = min(0.25, tau * 1.3)
        # LP
        M = 2*n - 1
        if n <= 100:
            counts = [1,5,20,M]
        elif n <= 220:
            counts = [1,5,20,60]
        else:
            counts = [1,5,20]
        max_count = 0
        for c in counts:
            if c > max_count: max_count = c
        max_count = min(max_count, M)
        pos_all = top_positions_from_vals(vals_obj, max_count)
        used = []
        for c in counts:
            cc = min(c, len(pos_all))
            if cc not in used:
                used.append(cc)
        shifts = get_shift_list(n)
        for cc in used:
            if time.time() > t_end: break
            active_shifts = []
            active_counts = []
            for p in pos_all[:cc]:
                active_shifts.append(int(shifts[p]))
                active_counts.append(float(vals_count[p]))
            h2, val2 = lp_line_search(h, active_shifts, active_counts, cur, target)
            if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2; cur = val2; stall = 0; tau = max(0.003, tau * 0.92)
                improved = True
                break
        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 60:
                break
            if stall == 30 or stall == 45:
                # larger random
                for noise in (0.1, 0.2, 0.35):
                    h2 = enforce_sum(h + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), target)
                    val2 = evaluate_raw(h2)
                    if np.isfinite(val2) and val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                        h = h2; cur = val2; stall = 0
                        break
                if stall == 0:
                    continue
        # if time low
    return h, evaluate_raw(h)
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog

DIRECT_LIMIT = 320
SMOOTH_STEPS = (1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.15, 0.1, 0.05)
LP_STEPS = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05)

SHIFT_CACHE = {}
_LP_AVAILABLE = True


def _determine_corr_sign():
    n = 6
    h = np.array([0.3, 0.9, 0.1, 0.6, 0.4, 0.2], dtype=float)
    b = 1.0 - h
    vals = np.correlate(h, b, mode="full")

    for k_sign in (1, -1):
        ok = True
        for p in range(2 * n - 1):
            k = k_sign * (n - 1 - p)
            if k >= 0:
                exp = float(np.dot(h[:n - k], b[k:]))
            else:
                r = -k
                exp = float(np.dot(h[r:], b[:n - r]))
            if abs(vals[p] - exp) > 1e-8:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            return k_sign
    return 1


CORR_K_SIGN = _determine_corr_sign()


def get_shift_list(n):
    if n not in SHIFT_CACHE:
        shifts = []
        for p in range(2 * n - 1):
            shifts.append(int(CORR_K_SIGN * (n - 1 - p)))
        SHIFT_CACHE[n] = shifts
    return SHIFT_CACHE[n]


def pool_key(item):
    return item[0]


def prune_pool(pool, keep=200):
    if len(pool) > keep:
        pool.sort(key=pool_key)
        del pool[keep:]


def enforce_sum(h, target, tol=1e-12):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    n = h.size

    for _ in range(30):
        diff = float(target) - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= tol:
            break
        step = diff / float(n)
        cand = h + step
        if float(np.min(cand)) >= 0.0 and float(np.max(cand)) <= 1.0:
            h = cand
            continue
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-14)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-14
        if not np.any(mask):
            break
        cnt = float(np.sum(mask))
        if cnt <= 0.0:
            break
        delta = diff / cnt
        h[mask] = h[mask] + delta
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    diff = float(target) - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            idx = int(np.argmax(room))
            if room[idx] > 0.0:
                h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            if h[idx] > 0.0:
                h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h


def project_box_sum(h, target):
    return enforce_sum(h, target)


def _c5_fft(h, dx):
    n = h.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    a = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    b = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(1.0 - h), L)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(a * b, L)[:m]
    np.maximum(corr, 0.0, out=corr)
    mx = float(np.max(corr))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return mx * dx


def evaluate_raw(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    if n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        mx = float(np.max(corr))
        if not np.isfinite(mx):
            return float("inf")
        return mx * dx
    return _c5_fft(h, dx)


def _c5_values(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    a = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    b = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(1.0 - h), L)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(a * b, L)[:m]
    np.maximum(corr, 0.0, out=corr)
    return corr


def exact_evaluate(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    if n <= 1000:
        corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        mx = float(np.max(corr))
        if not np.isfinite(mx):
            return float("inf")
        return mx * dx
    return _c5_fft(h, dx)


def is_valid_sum(h, target, tol=1e-7):
    s = float(np.sum(h))
    return abs(s - float(target)) <= tol


def top_positions_from_vals(vals, count):
    m = len(vals)
    if count <= 0:
        return []
    if count >= m:
        idx = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(vals, -count)[-count:]
        idx = idx[np.argsort(vals[idx])[::-1]]
    out = []
    for x in idx:
        out.append(int(x))
    return out


def grad_for_shift(h, k):
    n = h.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if k == 0:
        g[:] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
        return g
    if k > 0:
        r = k
        g[:n - r] += 1.0 - h[r:]
        g[r:] -= h[:n - r]
    else:
        r = -k
        g[r:] += 1.0 - h[:n - r]
        g[:n - r] -= h[r:]
    return g


def weighted_gradient_fvals(h, fvals, tau):
    n = h.size
    if tau <= 0.0:
        tau = 1e-6
    fvals = np.asarray(fvals, dtype=float)
    maxv = float(np.max(fvals))
    z = (fvals - maxv) / tau
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(z - zmax)
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w = np.zeros(len(fvals), dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(fvals))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= sw

    shifts = get_shift_list(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for p in range(len(w)):
        wp = float(w[p])
        if wp > 1e-10:
            g += wp * grad_for_shift(h, shifts[p])
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    return g


def try_step_projected(h, direction, cur_val, target, steps=None):
    if steps is None:
        steps = SMOOTH_STEPS
    n = h.size
    if direction.size != n or n == 0:
        return h, cur_val

    direction = np.array(direction, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(direction)):
        return h, cur_val

    direction -= float(np.mean(direction))
    orig_max = float(np.max(np.abs(direction))) if n > 0 else 0.0
    if orig_max <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(orig_max):
        return h, cur_val

    direction /= orig_max
    orig_abs = orig_max if orig_max < 1.0 else 1.0

    lam_max = 1.0
    pos = direction > 0.0
    if np.any(pos):
        frac = (1.0 - h[pos]) / direction[pos]
        if np.any(np.isfinite(frac)):
            mpos = float(np.min(frac[np.isfinite(frac)]))
            if mpos < lam_max:
                lam_max = mpos
    neg = direction < 0.0
    if np.any(neg):
        frac = h[neg] / (-direction[neg])
        if np.any(np.isfinite(frac)):
            mneg = float(np.min(frac[np.isfinite(frac)]))
            if mneg < lam_max:
                lam_max = mneg

    if lam_max <= 1e-12:
        return h, cur_val

    lams = []
    for f in steps:
        lam = float(f) * lam_max
        if lam > 0.0:
            lams.append(lam)
    for x in (orig_abs, 0.5 * orig_abs):
        if x > 0.0 and x <= lam_max + 1e-12:
            lams.append(float(x))

    best_val = cur_val
    best_h = h
    for lam in lams:
        if lam <= 0.0:
            continue
        cand = h + lam * direction
        cand = enforce_sum(cand, target)
        val = evaluate_raw(cand)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val - 1e-12:
            best_val = val
            best_h = cand
    return best_h, best_val


def lp_line_search(h, active_shifts, active_counts, cur_val, target):
    global _LP_AVAILABLE
    if not _LP_AVAILABLE:
        return h, cur_val
    n = h.size
    m = len(active_shifts)
    if m == 0:
        return h, cur_val

    A = np.zeros((m, n + 1), dtype=float)
    b = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    for i in range(m):
        g = grad_for_shift(h, active_shifts[i])
        A[i, :n] = g
        A[i, n] = -1.0
        b[i] = -float(active_counts[i])

    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
    Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
    beq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)
    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    c[n] = 1.0

    bounds = []
    for j in range(n):
        bounds.append((-float(h[j]), 1.0 - float(h[j])))
    bounds.append((0.0, 2.0 * float(n) + 1.0))

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            Aeq=Aeq,
            beq=beq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"disp": False},
        )
        if not getattr(res, "success", False):
            return h, cur_val
        delta = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return h, cur_val

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
        return h, cur_val
    if float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) < 1e-13:
        return h, cur_val

    h2, v2 = try_step_projected(h, delta, cur_val, target, steps=LP_STEPS)
    if v2 >= cur_val - 1e-12:
        h3, v3 = try_step_projected(h, -delta, cur_val, target, steps=LP_STEPS)
        if v3 < v2:
            h2, v2 = h3, v3
    return h2, v2


def threshold_impulse(h, target):
    n = h.size
    m = int(round(float(target)))
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if m >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(-h)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    out[order[:m]] = 1.0
    return out


def quantize_exact(h, target, qbits=None):
    n = h.size
    if qbits is None:
        if n <= 4096:
            qbits = 20
        else:
            qbits = 14
    q = 1 << int(qbits)
    hi = np.rint(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0) * float(q)).astype(np.int64)
    hi = np.clip(hi, 0, int(q))
    target_q = int(round(float(target) * float(q)))
    rem = target_q - int(np.sum(hi))

    if rem > 0:
        order = np.argsort(q - int_q if False else hi)
        order = order[::-1]  # largest slack first
        for idx in order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if rem <= 0:
                break
            add = min(int(q - hi[idx]), int(rem))
            if add > 0:
                hi[idx] += add
                rem -= add
    elif rem < 0:
        rem = -int(rem)
        order = np.argsort(hi)
        order = order[::-1]  # largest values first
        for idx in order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if rem <= 0:
                break
            sub = min(int(hi[idx]), int(rem))
            if sub > 0:
                hi[idx] -= sub
                rem -= sub

    if rem > 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(q - hi))
        hi[idx] = q
    elif rem < 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(hi))
        hi[idx] = 0

    return hi.astype(float) / float(q)


def make_random_uniform(n, rng):
    return enforce_sum(rng.random_sample(n), float(n) / 2.0)


def make_lowpass_candidate(n, rng, bandwidth, alpha, amp):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    bandwidth = int(max(1, bandwidth))
    amp = float(min(0.49, max(0.05, amp)))
    for f in range(1, bandwidth + 1):
        sc = 1.0 / (float(f) ** float(alpha))
        cph = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
        sph = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
        ang = 2.0 * math.pi * float(f) * x / float(n)
        u += cph * sc * np.cos(ang)
        u += sph * sc * 0.7 * np.sin(ang)
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
    if mx <= 1e-14:
        u = rng.random_sample(n) - 0.5
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
    if mx <= 1e-14:
        u = rng.random_sample(n) - 0.5
        mx = 1.0
    u = (amp * u) / mx
    h = 0.5 + u
    return enforce_sum(h, float(n) / 2.0)


def interpolate_parent(parent, n):
    n0 = parent.size
    if n0 == n:
        return np.array(parent, dtype=float, copy=True)
    x0 = (np.arange(n0, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n0))
    x1 = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return np.interp(x1, x0, parent)


def pool_add(pool, h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.ndim != 1 or h.size == 0 or (h.size % 2 != 0):
        return
    n = h.size
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return
    if float(np.min(h)) < -1e-6 or float(np.max(h)) > 1.0 + 1e-6:
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = enforce_sum(h, float(n) / 2.0)
    val = evaluate_raw(h)
    if np.isfinite(val):
        pool.append((float(val), h, int(n)))
        if len(pool) > 350:
            prune_pool(pool, 220)


def polish(h0, t_end, seed):
    h = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    h = enforce_sum(h, target)
    cur = evaluate_raw(h)
    if not np.isfinite(cur):
        return h, cur

    seed = int(seed) % 2147483647
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    tau = 0.08
    stall = 0

    while time.time() < t_end:
        if time.time() >= t_end:
            break

        vals_count = _c5_values(h)
        if vals_count.size != (2 * n - 1):
            break
        dx = 2.0 / float(n)
        vals_obj = vals_count * dx
        vals_obj = np.where(np.isfinite(vals_obj), vals_obj, -1.0)
        cur = float(np.max(vals_obj))
        if not np.isfinite(cur) or cur < -1e-12:
            break

        improved = False

        if time.time() < t_end - 0.5:
            g = weighted_gradient_fvals(h, vals_obj, tau)
            h2, v2 = try_step_projected(h, -g, cur, target, steps=SMOOTH_STEPS)
            if np.isfinite(v2) and v2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2
                cur = v2
                stall = 0
                tau = max(0.004, tau * 0.96)
                continue

        if stall > 2 and (stall % 5 == 0 or stall > 20):
            noise = 0.02 + 0.04 * min(1.0, float(stall) / 20.0)
            h2 = enforce_sum(h + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), target)
            v2 = evaluate_raw(h2)
            if np.isfinite(v2) and v2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2
                cur = v2
                stall = 0
                continue
            ht = threshold_impulse(h, target)
            vt = evaluate_raw(ht)
            if np.isfinite(vt) and vt < cur - 1e-12:
                h = ht
                cur = vt
                stall = 0
                continue
            if stall % 10 == 0:
                tau = min(0.25, tau * 1.3)

        m = 2 * n - 1
        if n <= 100:
            counts = [1, 5, 20, m]
        elif n <= 220:
            counts = [1, 5, 20, 60]
        else:
            counts = [1, 5, 20]

        max_count = 0
        for cc in counts:
            if cc > max_count:
                max_count = cc
        max_count = min(max_count, m)

        pos_all = top_positions_from_vals(vals_obj, max_count)
        used_counts = []
        for cc in counts:
            cc2 = min(cc, len(pos_all))
            if cc2 not in used_counts:
                used_counts.append(cc2)

        shifts = get_shift_list(n)
        for cc in used_counts:
            if time.time() > t_end:
                break
            active_shifts = []
            active_counts = []
            for p in pos_all[:cc]:
                active_shifts.append(int(shifts[p]))
                active_counts.append(float(vals_count[p]))
            h2, v2 = lp_line_search(h, active_shifts, active_counts, cur, target)
            if np.isfinite(v2) and v2 < cur - 1e-12:
                h = h2
                cur = v2
                stall = 0
                tau = max(0.003, tau * 0.92)
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 60:
                break
            if stall == 30 or stall == 45:
                for noise in (0.10, 0.20, 0.35):
                    h2 = enforce_sum(h + rng.normal(0.0, float(noise), n), target)
                    v2 = evaluate_raw(h2)
                    if np.isfinite(v2) and v2 < cur - 1e-12:
                        h = h2
                        cur = v2
                        stall = 0
                        break
                if stall == 0:
                    continue

    return h, evaluate_raw(h)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        b = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        b = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(b) or b <= 10.0:
        b = 120.0

    seed_int = int(seed) if seed is not None else 42
    final_margin = max(3.0, min(15.0, 0.02 * b))
    search_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, b - final_margin)

    parent = None
    try:
        parent_input = initial_h_values
    except Exception:
        parent_input = None
    if parent_input is not None:
        par = np.asarray(parent_input, dtype=float)
        if par.ndim == 1 and par.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(par)):
            if par.size % 2 != 0:
                par = interpolate_parent(par, par.size + 1)
            parent = par

    pool = []

    if parent is not None:
        nn = parent.size
        hpar = enforce_sum(parent, float(nn) / 2.0)
        pool_add(pool, hpar)
        pool_add(pool, threshold_impulse(parent, float(nn) / 2.0))
        pool_add(pool, quantize_exact(hpar, float(nn) / 2.0))

    if parent is not None:
        n_parent = parent.size
    else:
        n_parent = 70

    ns = [n_parent if n_parent % 2 == 0 else n_parent + 1]
    if b >= 900.0:
        ns.append(n_parent * 2 if (n_parent * 2) % 2 == 0 else n_parent * 2 + 1)
        ns.append(n_parent * 3 if (n_parent * 3) % 2 == 0 else n_parent * 3 + 1)
    elif b >= 400.0:
        ns.append(n_parent * 2 if (n_parent * 2) % 2 == 0 else n_parent * 2 + 1)

    final_ns = []
    for n in ns:
        if n % 2 != 0:
            n += 1
        if 10 <= n <= 500 and n not in final_ns:
            final_ns.append(int(n))
    if len(final_ns) == 0:
        final_ns = [70]

    gen_raw = t0 + max(5.0, min(180.0, 0.12 * b, 0.5 * b))
    gen_deadline = min(gen_raw, max(t0 + 2.0, search_deadline - 30.0))
    if gen_deadline < t0 + 1.0:
        gen_deadline = t0 + 1.0

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed_int % 2147483647)

    for n in final_ns:
        if time.time() > gen_deadline:
            break
        target = float(n) / 2.0

        if parent is not None:
            hp = interpolate_parent(parent, n)
            pool_add(pool, hp)
            pool_add(pool, threshold_impulse(hp, target))
            pool_add(pool, quantize_exact(enforce_sum(hp, target), target))

        max_trials = 120 if n <= 140 else 60
        for trial in range(max_trials):
            if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                break
            bandwidth = int(rng.choice([2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12]))
            alpha = 0.5 + rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)
            amp = 0.35 + rng.uniform(0.0, 0.14)
            pool_add(pool, make_lowpass_candidate(n, rng, bandwidth, alpha, amp))

        for trial in range(30):
            if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                break
            pool_add(pool, make_random_uniform(n, rng))

    prune_pool(pool, 220)
    pool.sort(key=pool_key)

    polished_ids = set()
    while time.time() < search_deadline:
        pool.sort(key=pool_key)
        chosen = -1
        for idx in range(len(pool)):
            if id(pool[idx][1]) not in polished_ids:
                chosen = idx
                break
        if chosen < 0:
            break

        remaining = search_deadline - time.time()
        if remaining < 30.0:
            break

        if chosen == 0:
            max_alloc = max(120.0, 0.50 * b)
        elif chosen == 1:
            max_alloc = max(60.0, 0.22 * b)
        elif chosen == 2:
            max_alloc = max(45.0, 0.12 * b)
        else:
            max_alloc = max(30.0, 0.06 * b)

        alloc = min(remaining - 5.0, max_alloc)
        if alloc < 10.0:
            alloc = min(remaining - 2.0, 10.0)
        if alloc <= 0.0:
            break

        t_end_pol = time.time() + alloc
        val0, h0, n0 = pool[chosen]
        polished_ids.add(id(h0))

        h_pol, v_pol = polish(h0, t_end_pol, seed_int + 1000 * chosen + n0)
        if np.isfinite(v_pol):
            pool_add(pool, h_pol)

        if time.time() < search_deadline - 30.0:
            for n2 in final_ns:
                if n2 != n0 and time.time() < search_deadline - 30.0:
                    h2 = interpolate_parent(h_pol, n2)
                    pool_add(pool, h2)
                    pool_add(pool, threshold_impulse(h2, float(n2) / 2.0))

        pool.sort(key=pool_key)

    pool.sort(key=pool_key)

    final_pool = []
    seen_ids = set()
    for idx in range(min(80, len(pool))):
        v, h, n = pool[idx]
        hid = id(h)
        if hid in seen_ids:
            continue
        seen_ids.add(hid)
        target = float(n) / 2.0
        h1 = enforce_sum(h, target)
        final_pool.append((exact_evaluate(h1), h1, int(n)))
        h2 = quantize_exact(h1, target)
        final_pool.append((exact_evaluate(h2), h2, int(n)))
        h3 = threshold_impulse(h1, target)
        final_pool.append((exact_evaluate(h3), h3, int(n)))
    final_pool.sort(key=pool_key)

    best_val_valid = float("inf")
    best_h_valid = None
    best_n_valid = 0
    best_val_any = float("inf")
    best_h_any = None
    best_n_any = 0

    for val, h, n in final_pool:
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            continue
        target = float(n) / 2.0
        if val < best_val_any:
            best_val_any = val
            best_h_any = h
            best_n_any = n
        if val < best_val_valid and is_valid_sum(h, target, 1e-7):
            best_val_valid = val
            best_h_valid = h
            best_n_valid = n

    if best_h_valid is None and best_h_any is not None:
        target = float(best_n_any) / 2.0
        hq = quantize_exact(best_h_any, target)
        vq = exact_evaluate(hq)
        if np.isfinite(vq):
            best_h_valid = hq
            best_val_valid = vq
            best_n_valid = best_n_any

    if best_h_valid is None and best_h_any is not None:
        best_h_valid = best_h_any
        best_val_valid = best_val_any
        best_n_valid = best_n_any

    if best_h_valid is None:
        h_fb = make_random_uniform(70, rng)
        v_fb = exact_evaluate(h_fb)
        return h_fb, float(v_fb), 70

    final_h = np.asarray(best_h_valid, dtype=float)
    final_val = exact_evaluate(final_h)
    if not np.isfinite(final_val):
        h_fb = make_random_uniform(70, rng)
        final_h = h_fb
        final_val = exact_evaluate(h_fb)

    return final_h, float(final_val), int(final_h.size)
```
